# Calculate the start of the current week and 8 weeks ago
# We need transactions from 8 weeks ago up to (but not including) the current week
transactions_last_8_weeks = Transactions.WHERE(
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) >= 1) &  # Exclude current week
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 8)    # Include last 8 weeks
).CALCULATE(
    week_number = 8 - DATEDIFF("weeks", date_time, DATETIME("now")) + 1,  # Week 1 is 8 weeks ago, Week 8 is last week
    day_of_week = DAYOFWEEK(date_time),
    is_weekend = (DAYNAME(date_time) == "Saturday") | (DAYNAME(date_time) == "Sunday")
)

# Group by week and calculate counts
result = transactions_last_8_weeks.PARTITION(
    name="weeks", 
    by=week_number
).CALCULATE(
    week_number = week_number,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(Transactions.CALCULATE(is_weekend_int = IFF(is_weekend, 1, 0)).is_weekend_int)
).ORDER_BY(week_number.ASC())

SELECT
  _s0.week_number,
  COALESCE(_s0.agg_0, 0) AS total_transactions,
  COALESCE(_s1.agg_1, 0) AS weekend_transactions
FROM _s0 AS _s0
LEFT JOIN _s1 AS _s1
  ON _s0.week_number = _s1.week_number
ORDER BY
  week_number

# Define the start and end of the 8-week period, excluding the current week.
start_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')
end_of_period = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Filter for stock transactions within the specified date range.
recent_stock_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(ticker.ticker_type) == 'stock') &
    (date_time >= start_of_period) &
    (date_time < end_of_period)
)

# For each transaction, determine its week and whether it occurred on a weekend.
transaction_details = recent_stock_transactions.CALCULATE(
    transaction_week = DATETIME(date_time, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYNAME(date_time), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))
)

# Group transactions by week and count the total and weekend transactions.
weekly_summary = transaction_details.PARTITION(name="weekly_groups", by=transaction_week).CALCULATE(
    week = transaction_week,
    total_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    weekend_transactions = SUM(is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

result = weekly_summary

# Get transactions from USA customers that occurred last week
usa_transactions_last_week = Customers.WHERE(
    LOWER(country) == LOWER('USA')
).transactions_made.WHERE(
    # Start of current week (Sunday)
    (date_time < DATETIME('now', 'start of week'))
    &
    # Start of last week (Sunday)
    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days'))
)

# Calculate the count and total amount
result = Broker.CALCULATE(
    num_transactions=COUNT(usa_transactions_last_week),
    total_amount=SUM(usa_transactions_last_week.amount)
)

# Define the start and end of the previous week, assuming the week starts on Sunday.
# 'start of week' with '-7 days' gives the start of last week.
# The end of last week is the start of the current week.
last_week_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days')
last_week_end = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')

# Filter transactions to include only those from USA customers within the last week.
last_week_usa_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(customer.country) == 'usa') &
    (date_time >= last_week_start) &
    (date_time < last_week_end)
)

# Calculate the total number of transactions and their total amount.
result = Broker.CALCULATE(
    number_of_transactions=COUNT(last_week_usa_transactions),
    total_transaction_amount=SUM(last_week_usa_transactions.amount)
)

# Filter transactions for buy transactions in the past 10 days
recent_buy_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'buy') & 
    (DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME('now')) <= 10)
)

# Group by ticker symbol and count transactions
ticker_buy_counts = recent_buy_transactions.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol = ticker.symbol
).PARTITION(name="ticker_groups", by=ticker_symbol).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol = ticker_symbol,
    num_buy_transactions = COUNT(Transactions)
)

# Get the top 2 most frequently bought tickers
result = ticker_buy_counts.TOP_K(2, by=num_buy_transactions.DESC())

# Step 1: Filter transactions to include only 'buy' types within the last 10 days,
# and calculate the ticker symbol for each.
recent_buy_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'buy') & 
    (DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME('now')) <= 10)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by ticker symbol, count the number of transactions
# in each group, and select the top 2 based on the count.
most_frequent_stocks = recent_buy_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="ticker_groups", by=(ticker_symbol)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    num_buy_transactions=COUNT(Transactions)
).TOP_K(2, by=num_buy_transactions.DESC())

# Get customers who joined in the last 6 months excluding current month
# Calculate the date range: from 6 months ago to the start of current month
recent_customers = Customers.WHERE(
    (join_date >= DATETIME('now', '-6 months', 'start of month')) &
    (join_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of month'))
).CALCULATE(
    join_month = JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(join_date), LPAD(MONTH(join_date), 2, '0'))
)

# Partition by join month to get customer signups per month
monthly_signups = recent_customers.PARTITION(name='months', by=join_month).CALCULATE(
    month = join_month,
    pmcs = COUNT(Customers)
)

# For PMAT, we need to calculate average transaction amount per month
# Get all transactions from these recent customers
customer_transactions = recent_customers.transactions_made.CALCULATE(
    transaction_month = JOIN_STRINGS('-', YEAR(date_time), LPAD(MONTH(date_time), 2, '0')),
    transaction_amount = amount
)

# Partition transactions by month and calculate average
monthly_transactions = customer_transactions.PARTITION(name='tx_months', by=transaction_month).CALCULATE(
    month = transaction_month,
    pmat = AVG(transactions_made.amount)
)

# Combine the results - we need to join PMCS and PMAT by month
# Since we need both metrics for each month, let's use a different approach
# Calculate both metrics in one query by partitioning customers by join month

result = recent_customers.PARTITION(name='signup_months', by=join_month).CALCULATE(
    month = join_month,
    pmcs = COUNT(Customers),
    pmat = AVG(Customers.transactions_made.amount)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Step 1: Filter transactions to 'sell' types within the last month and extract the ticker symbol for grouping.
sell_transactions_info = Transactions.WHERE(
    (LOWER(transaction_type) == 'sell') &
    (date_time >= DATETIME('now', '-1 month'))
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker.symbol
)

# Step 2: Partition the filtered transactions by ticker symbol.
# For each ticker group, calculate the sum of amount, tax, and commission.
ticker_groups = sell_transactions_info.PARTITION(
    name="ticker_groups",
    by=(ticker_symbol)
).CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    total_amount_from_sells=SUM(Transactions.amount),
    total_tax=SUM(Transactions.tax),
    total_commission=SUM(Transactions.commission)
)

# Step 3: Calculate the final SPM using the aggregated values from the previous step.
# This includes a check to prevent division by zero if the total amount is zero.
# The final result includes only the ticker symbol and the calculated SPM.
result = ticker_groups.CALCULATE(
    ticker_symbol=ticker_symbol,
    spm=IFF(
        total_amount_from_sells > 0,
        (total_amount_from_sells - (total_tax + total_commission)) / total_amount_from_sells * 100.0,
        0.0
    )
)

# Step 1: Extract month and symbol from daily prices
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol = ticker.symbol,
    month = JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0")),
    close = close,
    high = high,
    low = low
)

# Step 2: Partition by symbol and month to calculate monthly statistics
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(
    name="symbol_month", 
    by=(symbol, month)
).CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    avg_close = AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high = MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low = MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Calculate MoMC using PREV to get previous month's average
result = monthly_stats.CALCULATE(
    symbol = symbol,
    month = month,
    avg_close = avg_close,
    max_high = max_high,
    min_low = min_low,
    prev_avg_close = PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="Broker", default=0),
    momc = IFF(
        PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="Broker", default=0) == 0,
        0,
        (avg_close - PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="Broker", default=0)) / PREV(avg_close, by=(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC()), per="Broker", default=0)
    )
).CALCULATE(
    symbol,
    month,
    avg_close,
    max_high,
    min_low,
    momc
).ORDER_BY(symbol.ASC(), month.ASC())

# Step 1: Create a base collection with the necessary calculated fields: ticker symbol and a formatted month string for grouping and sorting.
price_info = DailyPrices.CALCULATE(
    symbol=ticker.symbol,
    month_str=JOIN_STRINGS("-", YEAR(date), LPAD(MONTH(date), 2, "0"))
)

# Step 2: Partition the daily prices by symbol and month to calculate the monthly aggregates.
# This creates a collection where each record contains the stats for a specific ticker in a specific month.
monthly_stats = price_info.PARTITION(name="monthly_groups", by=(symbol, month_str)).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month_str,
    avg_close=AVG(DailyPrices.close),
    max_high=MAX(DailyPrices.high),
    min_low=MIN(DailyPrices.low)
)

# Step 3: Partition the monthly stats by symbol. This allows us to use the PREV window function
# to get the previous month's data for each ticker separately.
# The 'per="ticker_groups"' argument in PREV ensures the calculation is contained within each ticker's data.
result = monthly_stats.PARTITION(name="ticker_groups", by=(symbol)).monthly_groups.CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=avg_close,
    highest_price=max_high,
    lowest_price=min_low,
    prev_avg_close=PREV(avg_close, by=month.ASC(), per="ticker_groups")
).CALCULATE(
    symbol=symbol,
    month=month,
    average_closing_price=average_closing_price,
    highest_price=highest_price,
    lowest_price=lowest_price,
    # Calculate MoMC, handling cases where the previous month's average is null or zero to avoid errors.
    MoMC=IFF(
        (prev_avg_close == None) | (prev_avg_close == 0),
        None,
        (average_closing_price - prev_avg_close) / prev_avg_close
    )
)

# First, prepare transaction data with customer state and ticker type
transaction_info = Transactions.CALCULATE(
    customer_state = customer.state,
    ticker_type = ticker.ticker_type
)

# Partition by the combination of customer state and ticker type
state_type_groups = transaction_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_type_combos", 
    by=(customer_state, ticker_type)
).CALCULATE(
    customer_state = customer_state,
    ticker_type = ticker_type,
    num_transactions = COUNT(Transactions)
)

# Get the top 5 combinations
result = state_type_groups.TOP_K(5, by=num_transactions.DESC())

[DEBUG] [Q24] Code generated by gemini:
# First, we access the Transactions collection and calculate the customer's state and the ticker's type for each transaction.
# These values are needed for partitioning.
transaction_info = Transactions.CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer.state,
    ticker_type_value=ticker.ticker_type
)

# Next, we partition the transactions by the combination of customer state and ticker type.
# For each group, we calculate the state, ticker type, and the total number of transactions.
top_combinations = transaction_info.PARTITION(
    name="state_type_combinations", by=(customer_state, ticker_type_value)
).CALCULATE(
    customer_state=customer_state,
    ticker_type=ticker_type_value,
    number_of_transactions=COUNT(Transactions)
)

# Finally, we select the top 5 combinations based on the number of transactions in descending order.
result = top_combinations.TOP_K(5, by=number_of_transactions.DESC())

# Filter transactions from the past 30 days (inclusive)
recent_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", date_time, DATETIME("now")) <= 30
)

# Add country information to each transaction
transactions_with_country = recent_transactions.CALCULATE(
    country = customer.country,
    amount = amount
)

# Group by country and calculate aggregates
country_stats = transactions_with_country.PARTITION(
    name="countries", 
    by=country
).CALCULATE(
    country = country,
    num_transactions = COUNT(Transactions),
    total_amount = SUM(Transactions.amount)
)

# Get top 5 countries by total transaction amount
result = country_stats.TOP_K(5, by=total_amount.DESC())

# Get high-value sales and their payments
high_value_payments = Sales.WHERE(sale_price > 30000).payment.CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    week_start=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(payment_date), (1, 7))  # Sunday=1, Saturday=7
)

# Filter to last 8 weeks excluding current week
current_week_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
eight_weeks_ago = DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-56 days')  # 8 weeks * 7 days

filtered_payments = high_value_payments.WHERE(
    (week_start >= eight_weeks_ago) & (week_start < current_week_start)
)

# Group by week and count payments
result = filtered_payments.PARTITION(name="weeks", by=week_start).CALCULATE(
    week=week_start,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(payment),
    weekend_payments_received=SUM(payment.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

# First, define the time window for the last 8 full calendar weeks.
time_window = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_of_this_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_window=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-8 weeks')
)

# Next, filter for sales over $30,000, get their associated payments within the time window,
# and calculate the starting date of the week for each payment, which will be used for grouping.
payments_to_group = time_window.Sales.WHERE(sale_price > 30000).payment.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= start_of_window) & (payment_date < start_of_this_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week_of_payment=DATETIME(payment_date, 'start of week')
)

# Finally, partition the payments by the week they occurred in.
# For each week, count the total number of payments and the number of payments that occurred on a weekend.
# The results are then ordered by week.
weekly_payment_counts = payments_to_group.PARTITION(name="weekly_groups", by=week_of_payment).CALCULATE(
    week=week_of_payment,
    total_payments_received=COUNT(PaymentsReceived),
    weekend_payments_received=COUNT(PaymentsReceived.WHERE(ISIN(DAYNAME(payment_date), ("Saturday", "Sunday"))))
).ORDER_BY(week.ASC())

result = weekly_payment_counts

# First, get cars that have been sold and are not in inventory
sold_cars_not_in_inventory = Cars.WHERE(
    # Car must have been sold
    HAS(sale_records) == 1
).WHERE(
    # Check the most recent inventory snapshot shows it's not in inventory
    inventory_snapshots.WHERE(
        RANKING(by=snapshot_date.DESC(), per="Cars", allow_ties=False) == 1
    ).SINGULAR().is_in_inventory == False
)

# Now get the sale information for these cars

    car_make=make,
    car_model=model,
    max_sale_price=MAX(sale_records.sale_price)
)

    name="make_model_groups", 
    by=(car_make, car_model)
).CALCULATE(
    make=car_make,
    model=car_model,
    highest_sale_price=MAX(Cars.sale_records.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(highest_sale_price.DESC())

# Get the date range for the last 6 months excluding current month
current_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
six_months_ago = DATETIME(current_month_start, '-6 months')

# Filter salespersons hired between 2022 and 2023
eligible_salespersons = Salespersons.WHERE(
    (YEAR(hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(hire_date) <= 2023)
)

# Get all sales from eligible salespersons with month truncation
sales_with_month = eligible_salespersons.sales_made.WHERE(
    (sale_date >= six_months_ago) & (sale_date < current_month_start)
).CALCULATE(
    salesperson_id=salesperson.salesperson_id,
    sale_month=DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month'),
    sale_price=sale_price
)

# Partition by month and salesperson to calculate metrics
result = sales_with_month.PARTITION(
    name="month_salesperson", 
    by=(sale_month, salesperson_id)
).CALCULATE(
    month=sale_month,
    salesperson_id=salesperson_id,
    pmsps=COUNT(sales_made),  # Count of sales
    pmsr=SUM(sales_made.sale_price)  # Sum of revenue
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Define the date range at the top level for access throughout the query.
# This establishes the start (6 months ago) and end (current month) of our window.
date_range_def = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-6 months'),
    end_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
)

# Start with all sales and calculate the month of each sale. This creates a "scaffold"
# of all months that have at least one sale, ensuring we can report on months with
# zero sales for our specific salesperson group.
sales_with_month = date_range_def.Sales.CALCULATE(
    sale_month = DATETIME(sale_date, 'start of month')
)

# Partition all sales by the calculated month. This groups all sales into monthly buckets.
monthly_groups = sales_with_month.PARTITION(name="monthly_sales", by=(sale_month))

# For each month group, calculate the required metrics.
# The filtering for salespersons (hired between 2022-2023) is applied *inside*
# the aggregation functions. This is the key to getting 0s for months that have
# sales, but not from the target salesperson group.
monthly_metrics = monthly_groups.CALCULATE(
    month = sale_month,
    # PMSPS: Count sales where the salesperson was hired in the target years.
    PMSPS = COUNT(Sales.WHERE(
        (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
    )),
    # PMSR: Sum sale_price for the same filtered group. Default to 0.0 if no such sales exist.
    PMSR = DEFAULT_TO(SUM(Sales.WHERE(
        (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) >= 2022) & (YEAR(salesperson.hire_date) <= 2023)
    ).sale_price), 0.0)
)

# Finally, filter the aggregated monthly results to our desired time window (last 6 months)
# and order them chronologically.
pmsps_and_pmsr_metrics = monthly_metrics.WHERE(
    (month >= start_date) & (month < end_date)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# We start with the PaymentsReceived collection, which inherently filters for sales that have payments.

).CALCULATE(
    car_make = make,

    # Get the date when the car went out of inventory (is_in_inventory = False)

        is_in_inventory == False
    ).SINGULAR().snapshot_date,

    sale_date = sale_records.SINGULAR().sale_date
).WHERE(

)

# Get payments from the previous ISO week
previous_week_payments = PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days')) &
    (payment_date < DATETIME('now', 'start of week'))
)

# Group by date and payment method, then calculate daily totals
result = previous_week_payments.PARTITION(
    name="daily_method_groups", 
    by=(payment_date, payment_method)
).CALCULATE(
    payment_date=payment_date,
    payment_method=payment_method,
    daily_total=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
).ORDER_BY(payment_date.ASC(), payment_method.ASC())

[DEBUG] [Q60] Code generated by gemini:
date_info = Dealership.CALCULATE(
    previous_week_start=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-7 days'),
    current_week_start=DATETIME('now', 'start of week')
)
payments_in_previous_week = date_info.PaymentsReceived.WHERE(
    (payment_date >= previous_week_start) & (payment_date < current_week_start)
)
daily_payment_groups = payments_in_previous_week.PARTITION(
    name="payment_groups", by=(payment_date, payment_method)
)
daily_totals = daily_payment_groups.CALCULATE(
    payment_day=payment_date,
    payment_type=payment_method,
    total_amount_received=SUM(PaymentsReceived.payment_amount)
)

    sale_year = YEAR(sale_date),
    sale_quarter = IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 3, 1,
                      IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 6, 2,
                          IFF(MONTH(sale_date) <= 9, 3, 4))),
    customer_state = customer.state,
    sale_price = sale_price
)

# Partition by state and quarter to calculate totals
quarterly_sales = sales_2023.PARTITION(
    name="state_quarter_groups", 
    by=(customer_state, sale_quarter)
).CALCULATE(
    state = customer_state,
    quarter = sale_quarter,
    quarter_start_date = IFF(sale_quarter == 1, "2023-01-01",
                            IFF(sale_quarter == 2, "2023-04-01",
                                IFF(sale_quarter == 3, "2023-07-01", "2023-10-01"))),
    total_sales = SUM(Sales.sale_price)
).ORDER_BY(state.ASC(), quarter.ASC())

result = quarterly_sales

    patient_id = patient_id,

)

    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME('now')) > 0) & 
    (DATEDIFF("months", start_dt, DATETIME('now')) <= 3)

    patient_id=patient_id,
    diag_id=diag_id
)

# Partition by month and calculate PMPD and PMTC
result = treatments_with_month.PARTITION(
    name="months", 
    by=treatment_month
).CALCULATE(
    month=treatment_month,

    PMTC=COUNT(Treatments)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# Define the start and end dates for the 12-month period preceding the current month.
start_period_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-12 months')
end_period_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter treatments to the specified period and create a field for the month of the treatment.
treatments_in_window = DermTreatment.Treatments.WHERE(
    (start_dt >= start_period_date) & (start_dt < end_period_date)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month_val=DATETIME(start_dt, 'start of month')
)

    name="monthly_groups", by=(treatment_month_val)
).CALCULATE(
    treatment_month=treatment_month_val,
    PMPD=NDISTINCT(Treatments.patient_id),
    PMTC=COUNT(Treatments)
).ORDER_BY(treatment_month.ASC())

    patient_id=patient_id,

)

# First, let's get treatments with their start year/month and count matching adverse events
treatments_with_matching_ae = Treatments.CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    start_year=YEAR(start_dt),
    start_month=MONTH(start_dt),
    matching_ae_count=COUNT(adverse_events.WHERE(
        (YEAR(reported_dt) == YEAR(start_dt)) & 
        (MONTH(reported_dt) == MONTH(start_dt))
    ))
)

# Now group by drug and sum up all matching adverse events
drug_ae_counts = treatments_with_matching_ae.PARTITION(
    name="drug_groups", 
    by=drug_id
).CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    total_matching_ae=SUM(treatments_with_matching_ae.matching_ae_count)
)

# Join with drug information and find the top drug
result = drug_ae_counts.CALCULATE(
    drug_id=drug_id,
    drug_name=Drugs.WHERE(drug_id == drug_id).SINGULAR().drug_name,
    num_adverse_events=total_matching_ae
).TOP_K(1, by=num_adverse_events.DESC())

    merchant_name=name,
    cpur=NDISTINCT(
        transactions_received.WHERE(

        ).WHERE(

[DEBUG] [Q94] Code generated by gemini:
merchant_cpur_rate = Merchants.CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=name,
    distinct_coupons_used=NDISTINCT(transactions_received.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'successful').coupon_id),
    distinct_transactions=NDISTINCT(transactions_received.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'successful').txid)
).CALCULATE(
    merchant_name=merchant_name,
    CPUR=IFF(distinct_transactions > 0, distinct_coupons_used / distinct_transactions, 0.0)
)

# Get notifications from US and Canada users for the last 3 weeks excluding current week
us_canada_notifications = Users.WHERE(
    (LOWER(country) == LOWER('US')) | (LOWER(country) == LOWER('Canada'))
).notifications.WHERE(
    # Filter for last 3 weeks excluding current week
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME('now')) >= 1) & 
    (DATEDIFF("weeks", created_at, DATETIME('now')) <= 3)
).CALCULATE(
    # Truncate created_at to start of week
    week_start = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    # Check if notification was sent on weekend (Saturday=7, Sunday=1)
    is_weekend = ISIN(DAYOFWEEK(created_at), (1, 7))
)

# Partition by week and calculate totals
result = us_canada_notifications.PARTITION(
    name="weekly_groups", 
    by=week_start
).CALCULATE(
    week_start = week_start,
    total_notifications = COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications = SUM(Notifications.is_weekend)
).ORDER_BY(week_start.DESC())

# Define the time boundaries for the last 3 weeks, excluding the current week.
# This makes the start and end dates available for the subsequent query.
ewallet_context = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    start_of_current_week=DATETIME('now', 'start of week'),
    start_of_period=DATETIME('now', 'start of week', '-3 weeks')
)

# Filter notifications to include only those sent within the defined time frame
# to users in the US or Canada. Then, for each notification, determine its
# corresponding week and whether it was sent on a weekend.
filtered_notifications = ewallet_context.Notifications.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_period) &
    (created_at < start_of_current_week) &
    ISIN(user.country, ('US', 'Canada'))
).CALCULATE(
    notification_week=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of week'),
    is_weekend=ISIN(DAYNAME(created_at), ('Saturday', 'Sunday'))
)

# Partition the filtered notifications by week and calculate the total and
# weekend notification counts for each week.
weekly_notification_summary = filtered_notifications.PARTITION(
    name='weekly_groups', by=(notification_week)
).CALCULATE(
    week_start_date=notification_week,
    total_notifications=COUNT(Notifications),
    weekend_notifications=SUM(is_weekend)
)

recent_balances = UserBalances.WHERE(
    DATEDIFF("days", updated_at, DATETIME("now")) <= 7
)

# Group by user and calculate average balance per user
user_avg_balances = recent_balances.PARTITION(

# Calculate the date range for last 2 months excluding current month
# Start of 2 months ago
start_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-2 months')
# Start of current month (exclusive end)
end_date = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')

# Filter transactions and calculate month
filtered_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
    (sender_type == 0) & 
    (created_at >= start_date) & 
    (created_at < end_date)
).CALCULATE(
    month = DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month'),
    sender_id = sender_id
)

# Partition by month and count distinct senders
result = filtered_transactions.PARTITION(
    name="monthly_groups", 
    by=month
).CALCULATE(
    month = month,
    pmdau = NDISTINCT(Transactions.sender_id)
).ORDER_BY(month.ASC())

# and create a column representing the month of the transaction.

).CALCULATE(

    user_id=uid,
    total_duration=SUM(
        sessions.WHERE(
            (session_start_ts >= "2023-06-01") & 
            (session_start_ts < "2023-06-08")
        ).CALCULATE(
            duration_seconds=DATEDIFF("seconds", session_start_ts, session_end_ts)

[DEBUG] [Q107] Code generated by claude:
# Get transactions from the previous month and calculate STR
result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    # Get the start of the previous month
    prev_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 month'),
    # Get the start of the current month (end of previous month)
    curr_month_start = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
).CALCULATE(
    # Filter transactions from the previous month
    prev_month_transactions = Transactions.WHERE(
        (created_at >= prev_month_start) & (created_at < curr_month_start)
    ),
    # Count successful transactions (case insensitive comparison)
    successful_transactions = COUNT(Transactions.WHERE(
        (created_at >= prev_month_start) & 
        (created_at < curr_month_start) & 
        (LOWER(status) == 'success')
    )),
    # Count total transactions
    total_transactions = COUNT(prev_month_transactions)
).CALCULATE(
    # Calculate STR as a percentage
    str_percentage = IFF(
        total_transactions > 0,
        100.0 * successful_transactions / total_transactions,
        0.0
    )
)

# Define the start of the current month and the start of the previous month
start_of_current_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month')
start_of_previous_month = DATETIME('now', 'start of month', '-1 month')

# Filter transactions to include only those from the previous month
transactions_in_previous_month = Ewallet.Transactions.WHERE(
    (created_at >= start_of_previous_month) & (created_at < start_of_current_month)
)

# Calculate the number of successful and total transactions, then compute the STR
str_result = Ewallet.CALCULATE(
    successful_transactions=COUNT(transactions_in_previous_month.WHERE(LOWER(status) == 'success')),
    total_transactions=COUNT(transactions_in_previous_month)
).CALCULATE(
    str_rate=IFF(total_transactions > 0, successful_transactions / total_transactions, 0.0)
)

    (YEAR(created_at) == 2023) &
    (LOWER(status) == 'completed') &
    (HAS(sending_user) == 1) &
    (LOWER(sending_user.status) == 'active')
).CALCULATE(
    transaction_month=DATETIME(created_at, 'start of month')

    by=(transaction_month)

    month=transaction_month,
    num_of_active_users=NDISTINCT(Transactions.sending_user.uid)

    sbtickerid AS _id,
    sbtickertype AS ticker_type
  FROM main.sbticker
  WHERE
    LOWER(sbtickertype) = 'stock'
), _s8 AS (
  SELECT
    COUNT() AS agg_0,
    DATE(
      sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime,
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME(sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime)) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    ) AS week_start_date
  FROM (VALUES
    (NULL)) AS _q_0
  JOIN main.sbtransaction AS sbtransaction
    ON sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime < DATE(
      'now',
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME('now')) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    )
    AND sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime >= DATE(
      'now',
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME('now')) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day',
      '-56 day'
    )
  JOIN _t5 AS _t5
    ON _t5._id = sbtransaction.sbtxtickerid
  GROUP BY
    DATE(
      sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime,
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME(sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime)) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    )
), _s9 AS (
  SELECT
    COUNT() AS agg_1,
    DATE(
      sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime,
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME(sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime)) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    ) AS week_start_date
  FROM (VALUES
    (NULL)) AS _q_1
  JOIN main.sbtransaction AS sbtransaction
    ON CASE
      WHEN CAST(STRFTIME('%w', sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime) AS INTEGER) = 0
      THEN 'Sunday'
      WHEN CAST(STRFTIME('%w', sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime) AS INTEGER) = 1
      THEN 'Monday'
      WHEN CAST(STRFTIME('%w', sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime) AS INTEGER) = 2
      THEN 'Tuesday'
      WHEN CAST(STRFTIME('%w', sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime) AS INTEGER) = 3
      THEN 'Wednesday'
      WHEN CAST(STRFTIME('%w', sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime) AS INTEGER) = 4
      THEN 'Thursday'
      WHEN CAST(STRFTIME('%w', sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime) AS INTEGER) = 5
      THEN 'Friday'
      WHEN CAST(STRFTIME('%w', sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime) AS INTEGER) = 6
      THEN 'Saturday'
    END IN ('Saturday', 'Sunday')
    AND sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime < DATE(
      'now',
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME('now')) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    )
    AND sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime >= DATE(
      'now',
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME('now')) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day',
      '-56 day'
    )
  JOIN _t5 AS _t11
    ON _t11._id = sbtransaction.sbtxtickerid
  GROUP BY
    DATE(
      sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime,
      '-' || CAST(CAST(STRFTIME('%w', DATETIME(sbtransaction.sbtxdatetime)) AS INTEGER) AS TEXT) || ' days',
      'start of day'
    )
)
SELECT
  _s8.week_start_date,

  ON _s8.week_start_date = _s9.week_start_date

  week_start_date
